# Docker

Every project across all 39 lists that ships a container image, a Dockerfile or a compose file — **2,200** of 11,884 distinct repos, deduplicated to one row per repo.

For building a container environment. Membership is evidence-based, not aspirational: a row is here because something in the repo or its README actually names a container.

[← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **2** of 4, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](docker.md) · **2** · [3](docker-3.md) · [4](docker-4.md) — [continue on page 3 →](docker-3.md)

Snapshot 2026-11-26. Stars, language, licence and last-push come from the GitHub API and drift daily.

Filter the same data in the browser on the [Atlas site](https://aaa.jeremyfhall.com/catalog/). Spreadsheet edition: retired. The last copy, built 2026-10-22, stays available but is no longer updated. The website is the maintained edition. Its last workbooks: [dark](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-DARK.xlsx) · [light](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-LIGHT.xlsx).
